1	# ADR: Domain Migration article-time.de → angel-crawford.de
2	
3	**Status:** Proposed
4	**Date:** 2026-05-12
5	**Owner:** Angel Crawford
6	**Related:** [`docs/1-planning/epics.md`](../1-planning/epics.md), Epic 2 (IndieWeb infrastructure)
7	
8	---
9	
10	## Context
11	
12	The current Hugo project — described in [README](../../README.md) as
13	"A personal Hugo blog being transformed into a **Digital Garden**" — is
14	hosted on GitHub Pages and currently serves under `article-time.de`.
15	The domain was originally purchased for an unrelated project: a
16	multi-author online magazine ("Article Time"), a dream that has since
17	been deliberately set aside in favour of a personal, single-author
18	digital garden.
19	
20	The project as currently shipped already implements the digital-garden
21	model technically (Growth Stages, Three-Tier Sorting, single-author
22	content, IndieWeb infrastructure planned in Epic 2 / Epic 7). What
23	mismatches is the **domain name**: `article-time.de` is a magazine
24	name, not a personal-identity domain.
25	
26	Separately, `angel-crawford.de` exists as a static Hugo profile card
27	("Webcard"), also hosted on GitHub Pages. It carries the canonical
28	online identity of Angel Crawford — it is linked from WhatsApp,
29	Discord, Facebook, Mastodon, Instagram, Spotify, Reddit, EA Sims
30	Gallery, GitHub, CodePen, and Stack Overflow. The Mastodon account
31	at `norden.social/@Angel_Crawford_ftw` is rel="me"-verified against
32	this domain. The profile card already carries `rel="me"` on all silo
33	links — what it does NOT have is full h-card microformat markup, but
34	that gap is acceptable because the profile card will be replaced by
35	the blog's home page during cutover (see Phase 3).
36	
37	The IndieWeb principle of "one domain = one identity" means these two
38	states are in tension: the personal-identity domain hosts only a
39	business card, while the personal-identity content lives under a
40	project-name domain.
41	
42	**No public content has been published yet on `article-time.de`.** The
43	site has been in development; no blog posts are linked from external
44	sources, no SEO history exists to preserve, no Webmentions have been
45	received on permalinks that need to be carried forward. This makes
46	the migration radically simpler than a typical domain move.
47	
48	---
49	
50	## Decision
51	
52	Migrate the digital garden from `article-time.de` to
53	`angel-crawford.de`. Both domains are hosted on GitHub Pages, so the
54	migration consists of two simple swaps:
55	
56	1. Point the blog's GitHub Pages deployment at `angel-crawford.de`
57	   instead of `article-time.de` (CNAME file change + GitHub Pages
58	   settings).
59	2. Replace what currently serves at `article-time.de` with a static
60	   maintenance/parking page (a single HTML file).
61	
62	The old profile card project on `angel-crawford.de` is retired in the
63	same act. No 301 redirects are needed (no inbound links to preserve).
64	No DNS work is needed (both domains already resolve via GitHub Pages
65	infrastructure).
66	
67	### What this decision does NOT change
68	
69	- **The project itself.** Code, Hugo theme, content, Epics, Stories,
70	  BMAD structure — all stay exactly as planned. The migration is a
71	  domain-level operation, not a re-architecture.
72	- **The transformation roadmap.** Epic 2 (engagement infrastructure),
73	  Epic 7 (POSSE), Epic 9 (polish) proceed as scheduled. They contain
74	  the IndieWeb work this ADR enables — they are not blocked by it.
75	- **The `article-time.de` registration.** The domain stays registered.
76	  It just stops serving the garden — it becomes a parking page.
77	- **The existing profile card on `angel-crawford.de`.** It is NOT
78	  touched during the build phase. It already serves its purpose (silo
79	  links + rel="me" for Mastodon verification) and will be replaced in
80	  one step during Phase 3, not gradually upgraded.
81	
82	### What it DOES change
83	
84	- The h-card eventually lives on `angel-crawford.de/` (the new home
85	  page = the blog's home page), not on a separate profile card domain.
86	- The Mastodon rel="me" verification target remains `angel-crawford.de`
87	  (already correct — no change needed there, only consistency
88	  reinforced).
89	- The Webmention endpoint registration with webmention.io moves to
90	  `angel-crawford.de` (currently `article-time.de` per Story 2.3 AC).
91	- Bridgy.fed account (Story 2.8) bridges Fediverse traffic to
92	  `angel-crawford.de`.
93	
94	---
95	
96	## Sequencing (Risk-Ordered)
97	
98	Migration runs in **three phases**, ordered so the project work
99	finishes naturally before the irreversible "one big event" happens
100	last, with a safety net (the project already has Maintenance-Mode
101	toggle infrastructure — see `README.md` → Maintenance Mode).
102	
103	### Phase 1 — Build IndieWeb infrastructure on the blog (Epic 2 / 7 / 9)
104	
105	**Goal:** Complete all IndieWeb features as already planned in the
106	BMAD epics, while the blog continues to be served at `article-time.de`.
107	
108	**Why first:** This is just normal Epic 2/7/9 work. The blog stays
109	on `article-time.de` throughout. No domain change yet. Stories 2.3
110	and 2.8 *will* need re-pointing in Phase 3, but they can be built and
111	tested under `article-time.de` first.
112	
113	**Critical:** The h-card is built INTO the blog theme (home page
114	template, `layouts/_partials/_base/`) — NOT into the old profile card
115	on `angel-crawford.de`. The old profile card stays as-is throughout
116	this phase. It already has `rel="me"` on silo links, which is enough
117	to keep Mastodon verification working. Its lack of h-card markup is
118	intentional: it would be wasted work, since the profile card is
119	scheduled for replacement, not upgrade.
120	
121	**Actions:** All Stories in Epic 2, plus Epic 7 if scheduled before
122	the cutover. **The current `article-time.de` configuration in Story
123	2.3 (`webmention.io/article-time.de/webmention`) is intentional during
124	this phase** — re-pointing happens in Phase 3.
125	
126	**Outcome:** Garden is feature-complete on `article-time.de`. Blog
127	home page carries full h-card markup. Old profile card on
128	`angel-crawford.de` is untouched.
129	
130	### Phase 2 — Cutover preparation
131	
132	**Goal:** Have everything ready so Phase 3 is one tag push.
133	
134	**Actions:**
135	- Prepare a static `index.html` for the future maintenance page on
136	  `article-time.de`. Plain HTML, no Hugo, no dependencies. Copy: e.g.
137	  "Diese Seite ist umgezogen nach angel-crawford.de." with a link.
138	- Audit Hugo config and templates for any hardcoded
139	  `https://article-time.de/...` strings. Hugo's `absURL` handles most
140	  via `baseURL`, but data files, partials, and content frontmatter can
141	  carry absolute URLs that won't auto-update.
142	- Locate the GitHub Pages custom-domain setting on the blog repo
143	  (`Settings → Pages → Custom domain`) and confirm the steps needed to
144	  change it.
145	- Locate the `CNAME` file in the blog repo (top-level, contains
146	  `article-time.de`) and confirm where to change it.
147	- Decide what happens with the old profile card repo: archive,
148	  delete, or convert to source for the maintenance page on
149	  `article-time.de`.
150	
151	**Outcome:** Phase 3 has nothing to figure out — just to execute.
152	
153	### Phase 3 — Cutover
154	
155	**Goal:** Switch the blog to `angel-crawford.de`. Old profile card
156	retired. `article-time.de` becomes a parking page.
157	
158	**Actions in order:**
159	1. Activate Maintenance-Mode on the live blog
160	   (`echo "" > .maintenance && git tag → push`). This is precautionary
161	   in case anyone hits the site during the swap.
162	2. Update `baseURL` in `config/_default/config.yaml` to
163	   `https://angel-crawford.de`.
164	3. Update the `CNAME` file in the blog repo from `article-time.de` to
165	   `angel-crawford.de`.
166	4. In the blog repo: `Settings → Pages → Custom domain` set to
167	   `angel-crawford.de`. Wait for "DNS check successful" green tick.
168	5. Retire the old profile card repo: either replace its content with
169	   the same blog build (if you went that route) or simply remove its
170	   custom-domain binding so `angel-crawford.de` resolves only to the
171	   blog.
172	6. Update webmention.io endpoint registration (re-register
173	   `angel-crawford.de`, update `<link rel="webmention">` target in
174	   blog templates if hardcoded).
175	7. Update Bridgy.fed domain verification.
176	8. Replace `article-time.de`'s deployment with the static
177	   maintenance/parking `index.html`. Concrete steps depend on your
178	   chosen route from Phase 2's "Decide what happens with the old
179	   profile card repo" — e.g. a new tiny repo with just the static
180	   HTML, with `article-time.de` set as its GitHub Pages custom domain.
181	9. Deactivate Maintenance-Mode on the blog.
182	10. Verify: Mastodon rel="me" still green, `indiewebify.me` passes
183	    h-card AND h-entry tests on `angel-crawford.de`, blog loads at new
184	    URL, `article-time.de` shows the parking page.
185	
186	**Outcome:** Garden lives at `angel-crawford.de`. `article-time.de`
187	shows a static "moved" page. h-card and h-entry are unified under one
188	canonical identity. The old profile card is retired in the same act
189	that brings the blog home — no transitional state.
190	
191	---
192	
193	## Alternatives Considered
194	
195	### Alternative 1 — Stay on article-time.de, accept the name as a brand
196	
197	**Pro:** Zero migration effort.
198	**Con:** `angel-crawford.de` (with its many inbound silo links and
199	verified Mastodon) stays a separate, weakly-connected island.
200	Violates the IndieWeb one-identity principle. Long-term, this means
201	two identities to maintain.
202	**Rejected because:** The whole point of the IndieWeb work in Epic 2
203	and 7 is identity coherence. Keeping the magazine-name domain would
204	permanently dilute that work.
205	
206	### Alternative 2 — Dual domains, one canonical
207	
208	**Pro:** Both URLs work, lower-risk migration.
209	**Con:** GitHub Pages doesn't natively serve one build under two
210	custom domains. Would mean two separate repos or build pipelines.
211	Adds permanent operational complexity.
212	**Rejected because:** The cutover in Phase 3 is so simple (no content
213	to preserve, no redirects needed) that the dual-domain workaround
214	would cost more than the cutover itself.
215	
216	### Alternative 3 — Upgrade the old profile card now, migrate later
217	
218	**Pro:** Could give `angel-crawford.de` a valid h-card sooner.
219	**Con:** The old profile card is on Hugo 0.156.0, separate from this
220	repo. Editing it means juggling two projects in parallel. And it's
221	work that gets thrown away in Phase 3, when the blog's home page
222	takes over.
223	**Rejected because:** No real benefit. The old profile card's
224	existing `rel="me"` is sufficient to keep Mastodon verification
225	working. Full h-card on `angel-crawford.de` is needed only once the
226	blog moves there — which happens in one step, not in stages.
227	
228	### Alternative 4 — Set up 301 redirects from article-time.de URLs
229	
230	**Pro:** Standard SEO best practice for domain migrations.
231	**Con:** No content is currently published on `article-time.de`, no
232	links exist that need to be preserved. The 301 setup would be
233	ceremony with no value.
234	**Rejected because:** There is literally nothing to redirect.
235	*If* this changes (some content goes live on `article-time.de`
236	before Phase 3 happens), revisit this alternative — at that point a
237	small Hugo-Aliases-based redirect generation becomes worthwhile.
238	
239	### Alternative 5 — Defer the domain question
240	
241	**Pro:** No decision pressure now.
242	**Con:** The identity mismatch keeps blocking IndieWeb decisions
243	(which domain does webmention.io register? which one does Bridgy.fed
244	bridge to?) and the BMAD Epic 2 work will encode the answer either
245	way. Deferring means encoding the *wrong* answer (article-time.de) in
246	Stories 2.3 and 2.8, and re-doing it later.
247	**Rejected because:** Cheaper to decide now and let Epic 2/7 work
248	forward toward the correct end state.
249	
250	---
251	
252	## Consequences
253	
254	### Positive
255	- IndieWeb identity becomes coherent under one canonical domain.
256	- All existing silo links (WhatsApp, Discord, etc.) automatically
257	  point at the right place — no link updates needed off-site.
258	- Mastodon rel="me" verification is preserved (same target domain).
259	- Future stories that touch IndieWeb infrastructure can assume
260	  `angel-crawford.de` as the canonical home.
261	- The old profile card project (Hugo 0.156.0) can be archived
262	  without further maintenance — no parallel work needed.
263	- No DNS work, no server config, no 301 redirect logic. The migration
264	  is essentially three text changes (`baseURL`, `CNAME`, custom-domain
265	  setting) plus two repo retirements.
266	
267	### Negative
268	- One-time cutover risk (mitigated by Maintenance-Mode and Phase 2
269	  preparation). Risk is small because both endpoints are static GitHub
270	  Pages sites with simple swap mechanics.
271	- The `article-time.de` registration becomes a "parking page" cost
272	  item. Acceptable, since the original dream may still resurface
273	  later under a different shape.
274	
275	### Neutral
276	- Epic 2 Stories 2.3 and 2.8 will be implemented with
277	  `article-time.de` initially, then re-pointed in Phase 3. The
278	  alternative — writing them with `angel-crawford.de` now and serving
279	  Webmention at a yet-to-exist endpoint — is worse. The re-point is a
280	  small, contained change.
281	- The old profile card on `angel-crawford.de` lacks h-card markup
282	  throughout Phase 1 and 2. This is acceptable: only Phase 3 makes
283	  `angel-crawford.de` the canonical home, and at that moment the
284	  h-card arrives with the blog home page.
285	
286	---
287	
288	## Implementation Tracking
289	
290	This ADR does **not** add new Epics or Stories. The migration
291	sequencing maps cleanly onto existing work:
292	
293	- **Phase 1** (build IndieWeb infrastructure on blog): Already
294	  tracked in [`epics.md`](../1-planning/epics.md). No change. The
295	  h-card-on-home-page work belongs in Epic 9 (Polish & Optimization) —
296	  see follow-up task below to confirm a story exists or add one.
297	- **Phase 2** (Cutover preparation): Could be a single Story under
298	  Epic 9 or its own micro-epic. Recommend: add as Story 9.13
299	  "Domain migration preparation" with the Phase 2 actions as ACs.
300	- **Phase 3** (Cutover): Recommend: Story 9.14 "Domain migration
301	  cutover" with the Phase 3 actions as ACs, marked as the final
302	  ship-blocker before declaring 1.0.
303	
304	---
305	
306	## Open Questions
307	
308	1. **What happens to the old profile card repo?** Three options:
309	   archive read-only, delete entirely, or repurpose as the source repo
310	   for the future `article-time.de` parking page. Decide before
311	   Phase 3 starts.
312	2. **Maintenance-Mode copy.** Phase 3 briefly shows the maintenance
313	   page on the blog during the swap. Default copy is "Wartung läuft"
314	   per README; cutover-specific copy not strictly needed since the
315	   maintenance window should be short (minutes, not hours). Default is
316	   fine unless preferred otherwise.
317	3. **Parking page copy on `article-time.de` after cutover.** What
318	   should the static `index.html` say? Suggestion: short bilingual
319	   note that this domain is no longer the canonical home, with a link
320	   to `angel-crawford.de`. Finalise wording before Phase 2 ends.
321	
322	---
323	
324	## Follow-up task for Claude Code
325	
326	**Verify h-card coverage in existing Epics before Phase 1 completes.**
327	
328	The README references planned IndieWeb work, and Epic 9 contains
329	several related stories: Story 9.7 (Twitter Cards), Story 9.10 (Author
330	Box with Socials on Single-Page), Story 9.12 (Social-Follow Icon Row).
331	None of these is explicitly "render a full h-card microformat on the
332	home page", which is the central artefact this ADR depends on.
333	
334	**Action items:**

337	   [`docs/1-planning/epics.md`](../1-planning/epics.md). Check whether

339	   - `class="h-card"` wrapper around the personal-identity block on

343	   - `class="p-note"` on the short bio,
344	   - `class="u-url"` with `rel="me"` on a link to `angel-crawford.de`
345	     itself (the canonical self-link).
346	2. Also check Story 9.2 (Schema.org Structured Data) — it is
347	   *different* from h-card (JSON-LD, not microformats) but their

353	4. If coverage is missing or partial: add a new story to Epic 9 with

356	   `indiewebify.me` h-card validation. Place it before Story 9.13

359	
360	The blog-side h-card is the artefact that replaces the old profile
361	card on `angel-crawford.de` during Phase 3 cutover. Without it,
362	Phase 3 ships an h-card-less site under the personal-identity domain,
363	which would defeat the point of the migration.

1399	# Epic 9: Polish & Optimization [Phase 2, Week 10-11]
1400

1403	**Value:** Professional quality, discoverability, inclusive design

1409	## Story 9.1: OG Image Template & Generation
1410	
1411	**As a** content creator
1412	**I want** unique OG images for each article with growth stage badge
1413	**So that** social media previews are rich and branded
1414	
1415	**FR Coverage:** FR-042
1416	
1417	**GitHub Issue:** [#116 Open Graph (Share Images)](https://github.com/AngelCrawford/blog/issues/116)
1418	
1419	**Acceptance Criteria:**
1420	1. OG image template created: `assets/images/og-template.png` (1200×630)
1421	2. Growth stage badge overlays added: `assets/images/badges/*.png`
1422	3. Hugo image processing partial: `layouts/_partials/og-image-generator.html`

1636	
1637	**Acceptance Criteria:**
1638	1. Heading render-hook updated at `layouts/_markup/render-heading.html` to inject anchor `<a>` after `<h2>`/`<h3>`/`<h4>`
1639	2. Anchor uses heading slug as `id` and `href="#slug"`

1652	**Pre-Spec Notes (from prior-story reviews):**
1653	- **Replace, don't add.** `layouts/_markup/render-heading.html` already exists and injects an anchor `<a class="anchor-link">` with a Remix `links-fill` `<svg class="remix">` after the heading text. The current state is visually broken — no `.anchor-link` / `.remix` CSS exists in `assets/scss/`, so the chain-link glyph renders at the SVG's intrinsic size (oversized, always visible, no opacity/hover behaviour) right next to every `<h2>`/`<h3>` on every page (visible on `/pages/datenschutz/` per 2026-05-09 screenshot review). This story must **replace** the existing markup or add the missing styles — do NOT assume a clean slate. Confirm AC #7 (no anchors on `<h1>`) by adding `{{ if gt $level 1 }}` or similar guard, since the current implementation injects on ALL levels.

Story 9.12's `params.social`-driven render replaces this with a structured set (Mastodon, Threads, GitHub, …). When wiring 9.12, **delete** the hardcoded line in `head.html` (and its 6-line preceding comment block) so the only source of `rel="me"` links is the social-follow partial — avoids duplicate GitHub `rel="me"` entries and keeps the IndieAuth identity surface in one place.

Adding one was deliberately deferred because this story removes the shim being tested — a test added at 2.3 would have created churn here. When 9.12 lands and the shim is deleted, no test cleanup is required; the absence is correct. Add a fresh assertion targeting the new `params.social`-driven output if AC coverage demands one.

421	## Story 2.8: Bridgy.fed Setup & Verification
422	
423	**As a** content creator
424	**I want** Mastodon (and Fediverse) replies, boosts, and likes to flow into my site as webmentions
425	**So that** federated engagement actually shows up in the webmention display, not just the small IndieWeb-blog subset
426	
427	**FR Coverage:** FR-011 (extends Webmention Endpoint Setup) — Bridgy.fed is the bridge that makes my webmention.io endpoint receive non-Indie traffic. Without it, the receive-side stories (2.3, 2.4, 3.2) only see mentions from blogs that natively send webmentions.
428	
429	**GitHub Issue:** [#145 IndieWeb - Webmentions & Pingbacks](https://github.com/AngelCrawford/blog/issues/145) (gemeinsam mit Stories 2.3 + 2.4 — issue erst schließen wenn alle drei done sind)
430	
431	**Source:** Gap identified during Story 2.5 implementation review. `ux-design-specification.md` lines 711–712, 947, 1624 reference Bridgy as planned infrastructure for "Mastodon → Webmention conversion", but no story implements the setup. Epic 7 covers OUTGOING POSSE (my site → Mastodon), not INCOMING bridging. Bridgy.fed (`https://fed.brid.gy/`) is the canonical IndieWeb bridge for incoming federated engagement.
432	
433	**Acceptance Criteria:**
434	1. Bridgy Fed account connected to a chosen Mastodon (or Bluesky) handle representing article-time.de — handle and instance documented in `docs/technical/runbook.md` or equivalent so a future operator knows where the bridge lives.
435	2. Domain verification with Bridgy Fed completed (rel="me" link or `.well-known/webfinger` — choose whichever Bridgy currently requires; document the chosen path).
436	3. End-to-end smoke test: a public Mastodon post that mentions or links to an article on article-time.de produces a webmention visible in the webmention.io dashboard within Bridgy's documented latency window. Document the test post URL + observed latency.
437	4. Privacy policy (`content/pages/datenschutz.md` → `## Webmentions` section) extended to disclose Bridgy.fed as an additional processor sitting between the originating Fediverse instance and webmention.io. Mention the operator (Ryan Barrett / Bridgy) and the data flow (public Fediverse activity → Bridgy → webmention.io → my site).
438	5. README or runbook entry documents how to disconnect / pause the bridge if needed (account suspension, abuse handling, opt-out for harassed mentions).
439	6. CSP review: confirm no new `connect-src` / `img-src` allow-list entries are required (Bridgy posts to webmention.io which is already allow-listed; sender avatars come from arbitrary federated instances which are already covered by `img-src https:`).
440	7. Bridgy operational status page link added to the runbook (if Bridgy goes down, mentions queue or are dropped — operator needs visibility).
441	
442	**Prerequisites:** Story 2.3 (webmention endpoint must exist for Bridgy to POST to)
443	
444	**Dependencies:** Story 3.2 (webmention processing script) — **soft dependency**: Bridgy can be wired up before 3.2 lands; mentions accumulate at webmention.io and are surfaced once 3.2's fetch-and-write loop runs.
445	
446	**Implementation Note:** Setup at `https://fed.brid.gy/` is largely UI-driven (~5 minutes of clicks). The story-card weight is in (a) deciding the Fediverse identity (which Mastodon instance or Bluesky handle to claim), (b) updating the privacy policy honestly, (c) writing the runbook entry so this isn't a one-person tribal-knowledge item, and (d) the smoke test that proves the loop actually closes. This is an **infrastructure-only story** — no code change to the Hugo site is strictly required (privacy-policy update is content-only). The Bridgy account itself is the deliverable.
447	
448	**Out of Scope:**
449	- Bridgy classic (Twitter) — Twitter killed Bridgy's API access; service is defunct. Don't attempt.
450	- Bluesky bridging — Bridgy supports it, but choose Mastodon-only for the initial story to keep the AC list tractable. Bluesky can be a follow-up note in completion notes.
451	- Outgoing POSSE (mein Blog → Mastodon-Posts) — that's Epic 7's territory (Stories 7.1, 7.2). Story 2.8 is incoming-only.
452	
453	**Effort:** 0.5 days

456	
457	## Story 2.9: Epic 2 Hardening & Follow-ups
458	
459	**As a** maintainer
460	**I want** the security, robustness, and CI/UX gaps that Epic 2 reviews tagged `[Review][Defer]` closed in one bundled story
461	**So that** the engagement infrastructure shipped in 2.1–2.6 is hardened against known edge cases without each fix dragging its own ad-hoc PR or being forgotten
462	
463	**FR Coverage:** None directly — bundled hardening story collecting deferred review items from done-stories 2.2, 2.4, and 2.6.
464	
465	**Source:** Items collected from Epic 2 done-stories' Review Findings that were tagged `[Review][Defer]` and explicitly out-of-scope at original-story implementation time (each story scoped to 0.5–1 day; security/robustness/CI follow-ups bundled here instead of expanding scope per-story). Originating entries: `2-2-heart-button-component.md` (items "reflect.IsMap" and "storageKey baseURL"), `2-4-webmention-display-component.md` (adversarial XSS fixture), `2-6-daily-rebuild-github-actions-workflow.md` (javascript:-URI XSS in webmention links, issue dedup, failing-step name, context.sha for cron, markdown injection).
466	
467	**Acceptance Criteria:**
468	1. **Security: `javascript:` / `data:` URI guard for webmention author + source links.** `layouts/_partials/widgets/webmention-group.html` (currently around lines 20–34) emits `<a href="{{ .author_url }}">` and `<a href="{{ .url }}">` without scheme filtering — Hugo's auto-escape covers HTML injection but **not** protocol injection, so a webmention.io payload with `author_url: "javascript:alert(1)"` would render as an executable link in production. Add an allowlist filter that emits `href` only if the URL scheme is `http://`, `https://`, or `mailto:`; otherwise fall through to the plain-text/`<span>` branch already established by Story 2.4's review patch. Add a build-smoke assertion covering the guard (a fixture entry with `author_url: "javascript:alert(1)"` must render as a `<span>`, not an `<a href>`).
469	
470	2. **Robustness: heart-button `reflect.IsMap` future-proofing.** `layouts/_partials/widgets/heart-button.html:27` currently branches on `{{ if reflect.IsMap . }}` to detect the dict-context card-invocation pattern (vs. single-page Page-context invocation). In a future Hugo version, `reflect.IsMap` may return true for Page objects, causing wrong-branch render and broken cards. Replace with a stricter positive check that the context actually carries the expected dict keys — e.g. `{{ if and (reflect.IsMap .) (isset . "page") }}` — so only true dict contexts hit the card branch.
471	
472	3. **Robustness: hearts.js `storageKey` baseURL-independence.** `assets/js/hearts.js` (around line 25) builds the localStorage key from `data-article` which is rendered as `$page.RelPermalink`. If `baseURL` ever changes (subpath deploy, custom-domain migration, prod→staging mirror), `RelPermalink` shifts and every existing `hearted-…` entry silently orphans → users lose visible hearted-state. Stabilise the key: either use `window.location.pathname` directly (browser-side resolved, never carries baseURL prefix), or strip a known prefix from the stored `data-article` before use. Add a code comment explaining the design intent so future-maintainer doesn't "fix" it back to the simpler raw form.
473	
474	4. **CI: GitHub Issue deduplication for persistent workflow failures.** `.github/workflows/daily-rebuild.yml` and `.github/workflows/third-party-asset-monitor.yml` currently create a new GitHub Issue on every failed run — persistent failures spawn one issue per cron tick (daily / weekly). Add search-first-or-reopen logic: query for an open issue with the same title (e.g. `daily-rebuild failed: <date>` → match on `daily-rebuild failed`); if found, append a comment ("Failure recurred on `<date>`, run: `<url>`") instead of opening a duplicate. If the existing issue is closed, reopen + comment. Net effect: an issue tracker that surfaces *one* item per persistent failure mode, not N items per N days.
475	
476	5. **CI: Failure-notification body quality.** Two improvements to the Notify-on-failure step bodies in both workflows:
477	   - **Include the failing step name** (not just "check the run logs"). Capture via `${{ steps.<id>.conclusion }}` or the GitHub API call. Goal: the issue body shows e.g. "Failed step: `Build Hugo site`", short-circuiting the operator's drill-down from issue → run → step.
478	   - **Use the actually-built commit SHA, not `context.sha`.** For cron builds the workflow checks out the latest tag, but `context.sha` resolves to HEAD-of-main at schedule time — misleading when investigating a build failure against tag content. Resolve the actual checkout SHA (`steps.checkout.outputs.commit` or equivalent) and embed that instead.
479	
480	6. **CI: Markdown-injection sanitization in issue bodies.** `FAILED_LIST` and failure messages in `.github/workflows/third-party-asset-monitor.yml`'s Notify-on-drift script are embedded verbatim — URLs with Markdown control chars (`_`, `*`, backticks, `[]`) render as unintended formatting in the rendered issue. Wrap embedded URLs in backticks (or run a small escape pass over Markdown control chars) so they render literally. Cosmetic; no security impact (operator-curated URLs, not attacker-controlled).
481	
482	7. **Test quality: adversarial XSS fixture for webmention build-smoke test.** `tests/build/build-smoke.test.mjs` (around line 1661 — Story 2.4 AC #8 XSS auto-escape test) currently uses clean fixture data with no HTML payloads, so the assertion "Hugo auto-escape is active" is technically unverified. Add one fixture entry to `data/webmentions_by_article.json` with `content: "<script>alert(1)</script>"` (and an analogous payload for `author`) and explicit assertions that the rendered HTML contains the escaped form `&lt;script&gt;alert(1)&lt;/script&gt;` and does **NOT** contain the raw `<script>` tag. Note: Story 2.4's [Defer] entry routed this to "Playwright / Story 3.2"; the build-smoke fixture is a strictly additive layer that does not block the Playwright/3.2 work.
483	
484	**Prerequisites:** Stories 2.2, 2.4, 2.6 (all `done`).
485	
486	**Dependencies:** None — all changes are localised to files already shipped; no new infra, no new external dependencies.
487	
488	**Implementation Note:** All seven ACs are independent and can ship in any order. Suggested PR-splitting if Angel prefers smaller PRs:
489	- **(a)** AC #1 (security — javascript: URI guard) — should ship first; it's the only security-grade item.
490	- **(b)** ACs #2 + #3 (heart-button + hearts.js robustness) — same file family, small.
491	- **(c)** ACs #4 + #5 + #6 (workflow notification quality) — same files (`.github/workflows/*.yml`), need a `workflow_dispatch` validation run per change.
492	- **(d)** AC #7 (build-smoke fixture) — touches only `tests/` and `data/`, independent.
493	All seven fit in one PR if Angel prefers a single Epic-2-hardening commit cluster.
494	
495	**Out of Scope** (each tracked in `docs/backlog.md` — not for this story):
496	- Heart visual design unification (backlog row "Design für Heart an allen Stellen") — UX redesign, not a hardening fix.
497	- Webmention visual design unification (backlog row "Design für Webmentions an allen Stellen") — same reasoning.
498	- Shared Hugo-build test fixture (backlog row "Redundant full hugo production builds") — test-infra refactor, not hardening.
499	- GitHub Actions version drift (backlog row "GitHub Actions version drift in `daily-rebuild.yml`") — dependency-bump cluster, validated per-action via `workflow_dispatch`, separate effort.
500	- Lighthouse audit (backlog row "Lighthouse Check und Anpassungen") — audit/measurement story.
501	- i18n migration (backlog row "Site-wide audit + migration of hardcoded UI strings to Hugo i18n") — cross-cutting refactor, not Epic-2-scoped.
502	- TOC frontmatter toggle (backlog row "TOC frontmatter toggle") — unrelated to Epic 2.
503	
504	**Effort:** 1–1.5 days (seven small fixes spanning ~6 files: `webmention-group.html`, `heart-button.html`, `hearts.js`, `daily-rebuild.yml`, `third-party-asset-monitor.yml`, `build-smoke.test.mjs`, fixture `data/webmentions_by_article.json`).

Volles Bild. Hier die Checklist-Befunde kompakt — bevor wir zu den konkreten Edit-Proposals gehen.

## Checklist-Findings (Section 1–4)

### §1 Trigger & Context
- **Trigger:** Domain-Identity-Mismatch zwischen `article-time.de` (Magazin-Name) und `angel-crawford.de` (Personal-Identity, alle Silo-rel="me"-Links). Voll dokumentiert in `adr-domain-migration.md`.
- **Kategorie:** Strategischer Pivot (Magazin-Dream verworfen, Digital-Garden ist die echte Mission).

### §2 Epic Impact
- **Epic 2 (Eng. Infrastructure):** Stories 2.3 + 2.8 enthalten hardcoded `article-time.de` (AC1/AC2 von 2.3, AC1/AC3 von 2.8). Per ADR-Decision: **Phase-1-Wahrheit** — bleibt bewusst so, wird in Phase 3 re-pointed. Empfehlung: einen ADR-Verweis als Pre-Spec Note einfügen, damit Implementierer den Kontext sieht.
- **Epic 3 (Story 3.2):** Hardcoded `article-time.de` im curl-Befehl (AC1). Gleiche Behandlung.
- **Epic 9:** 3 Erweiterungen nötig — **neue h-card Story (9.13), Migration Prep (9.14), Cutover (9.15)**.

### §3 Artifact Impact
- **PRD:** 14 Treffer auf `article-time.de` in 5 PRD-Files. Pragma: PRD ist v1.0 vom 2025-11-14, beschreibt den Stand der Solutioning-Gate. Vorschlag: **PRD nicht retroaktiv editieren** (würde Solutioning-Gate-Report invalidieren) — stattdessen ADR-Verweis in `prd/05-technical-architecture.md` als Header-Note.
- **Architektur:** 3 Files (`architecture.md`, `digital-garden-integration-architecture.md`, `development-guide.md`) referenzieren `article-time.de`. Gleiche Pragma — Verweis-Note statt Edit.
- **UX-Spec:** Aus dem Grep heraus offenbar nicht direkt betroffen (würde aber Phase 3 berühren).
- **CI/Hugo-Config:** `CNAME`, `config/_default/config.yaml` baseURL — gehören in Story 9.15 AC, nicht in diese Course-Correction.

### §4 Path Forward
- **Empfohlen: Option 1 (Direct Adjustment) – Hybrid.**
  - **Hybrid-Anteil:** Drei neue Stories in Epic 9 + leichte Pre-Spec Notes in Stories 2.3 / 2.8 / 3.2 / 9.8. Keine PRD-/Architektur-Edits (nur Header-Verweis).
- **Effort:** Low–Medium (nur Doku, kein Code).
- **Risiko:** Low. ADR ist self-contained und Phase-1-Work hängt nicht davon ab.

### h-card Coverage Check (ADR-Followup)
- **Story 9.2 (Schema.org JSON-LD):** Nein. JSON-LD ≠ Microformats2; deckt Article-Schema, nicht h-card-Homepage-Identity.
- **Story 9.10 (Author-Box):** Nein. Rendert am Artikel-Footer mit Author-Taxonomy-Term, nicht Site-Owner h-card auf Homepage. Microformat-Klassen nirgends in AC erwähnt.
- **Story 9.12 (Social-Follow):** Nein. Header/Footer-Icon-Row mit `rel="me"`-Links, KEIN h-card-Wrapper auf Homepage, kein `p-name`/`u-photo`/`p-note`.
- **Verdict:** Es existiert KEINE Story für h-card-on-home-page. Eine neue Story ist erforderlich vor Phase 3.

## Edit-Proposal 1 — Story 9.13 (NEU)

**Insertion-Punkt:** `epics.md` zwischen Zeile 1740 (Ende Story 9.12 Pre-Spec Notes) und Zeile 1742 (`# Epic Summary Table`). Folgt direkt auf Story 9.12.

````markdown
## Story 9.13: h-card on Home Page

**As a** site owner
**I want** a full h-card microformat on the home page identifying me as the canonical site author
**So that** IndieWeb tooling (webmention.io, Bridgy.fed, Mastodon rel="me" verification, indiewebify.me) treats the personal-identity domain as a coherent IndieWeb identity surface

**FR Coverage:** None directly — IndieWeb identity infrastructure that the engagement/POSSE epics depend on. Closest match: FR-011, FR-012 (webmentions assume identity coherence).

**GitHub Issue:** TBD (fits under [#124 IndieWeb](https://github.com/AngelCrawford/blog/issues/124) umbrella).

**Source:** Gap identified in `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` § Follow-up task. ADR concluded that no existing story (9.2 Schema.org JSON-LD, 9.10 Author-Box on article pages, 9.12 Social-Follow icon row) covers full h-card microformat markup on the home page. The blog-side h-card is the artefact that replaces the old profile card on `angel-crawford.de` during the Phase 3 cutover (Story 9.15). Without it, Phase 3 ships an h-card-less site under the personal-identity domain.

**Acceptance Criteria:**
1. Personal-identity block on the home page wrapped in `class="h-card"`. Block contains: display name, profile photo, short bio, canonical self-link.
2. Display name marked with `class="p-name"`.
3. Profile photo marked with `class="u-photo"` (use existing site avatar; do not introduce a new asset).
4. Short bio marked with `class="p-note"`.
5. Canonical self-link rendered as `<a class="u-url" rel="me" href="https://angel-crawford.de/">…</a>`. Absolute URL required (rel="me" round-trip validation). Phase-1 reality: the AC value stays `https://angel-crawford.de/` even while the site is served from `article-time.de` — the link points at the future canonical home (consistent with the Phase 3 cutover target).
6. Markup added to the existing home-page template (`layouts/index.html` or `layouts/home.html` — locate existing template; do NOT introduce a new template). If the home page currently has no author intro block, add one minimally; do NOT redesign the page.
7. Content (name, bio text, photo path) sourced from `config/_default/params.yaml` (new `params.identity` block) or from a single site-default location — NOT hardcoded in the template.
8. `indiewebify.me` h-card test passes against the home page URL during implementation (run against whatever preview/branch URL serves the build — validation tests the markup, not the live domain).
9. Visually unobtrusive — the h-card markup decorates the existing/new home-page author intro. No CSS-visible redesign required (only `class` and `rel` attributes are added).
10. No CSP changes required (no new external dependencies).

**Prerequisites:** None.

**Dependencies:**
- Soft coordination with Story 9.12 (Social-Follow Icon Row). Once 9.12 lands, the `params.social`-driven `rel="me"` silo links should sit INSIDE the h-card wrapper to count as h-card-relative rel="me" links. If 9.13 lands before 9.12: implement only the canonical self-link (AC5); the silo `rel="me"` links are added by 9.12 inside the same wrapper.
- Soft coordination with Story 9.10 (Author-Box on article pages). 9.10 renders an author block at article footer (`<aside>`), not on the home page — no direct conflict. If 9.10 ALSO adopts h-card classes for the per-article author, that is valid microformats2 (multiple h-cards per site are allowed).

**Effort:** 0.5 days

**Pre-Spec Notes:**
- **Single-author identity surface.** The home-page h-card identifies the ONE site owner. This story is unaffected by the separate Multi-Author → Co-Author decision (deferred to its own ADR) — per-article co-author markup is a property of the h-entry on article pages (Story 9.10 / 9.2 territory), not of this home-page h-card.
- **Self-link absolute, not relative.** AC5 must use the absolute URL of the canonical home, not `/`. Microformats2 parsers and IndieWeb tooling do resolve relative URLs in most cases, but rel="me" round-trip validators have known issues with relative `href` — absolute keeps the verification path robust.
- **Phase-1 reality.** This story can be implemented and validated under `article-time.de` (the canonical-target `rel="me"` href is `angel-crawford.de` regardless of where the page is currently served; indiewebify.me validates the markup, not the live domain). Cutover (Story 9.15) inherits the working h-card without further changes to the markup.
````

Verifikation:
- **Keine h-card im Codebase.** Grep auf `h-card|p-name|u-photo|p-note|u-url|h-entry|p-author|microformat` ergab keine echten Treffer (nur Bulma/SCSS und unrelated BMAD-Tooling). Erstanlage, kein Refactor.
- **Base-Layout-Struktur:** `layouts/baseof.html` + `layouts/_partials/_base/{head,hero,navigation,footer,seo,...}.html`. Der **Footer-Partial** (`footer.html`) ist die natürliche Heimat für eine site-wide h-card — er rendert auf jeder Seite (inkl. `/`), hat bereits eine identity-relevante Sektion (Author-Param, follow-icons, copyright mit Author-Name).

## Edit-Proposal 1 (revidiert) — Story 9.13

**Deltas zum ersten Draft:**
- **Story-Titel:** `h-card on Home Page` → `Representative h-card in Base Layout` (IndieWeb-Begriff; präziser, weil die h-card im Footer-Partial site-wide rendert, nicht nur auf `/`)
- **AC5 (rel="me" Self-Link auf `angel-crawford.de`):** **gestrichen** → verlagert in Story 9.15 als Post-Cutover-Validation (Self-Link nutzt `{{ .Site.BaseURL }}`, baseURL-Switch in 9.15 erledigt den Domain-Wechsel automatisch).
- **AC5 (neu):** Self-Link rendert als `<a class="u-url" rel="me" href="{{ .Site.BaseURL }}">…</a>`. Phase 1: Hugo löst auf `article-time.de`. Phase 3: nach `baseURL`-Update in 9.15 löst auf `angel-crawford.de`. Kein Code-Touch in 9.15 nötig.
- **AC6 (umformuliert):** Markup im **Footer-Partial** `layouts/_partials/_base/footer.html` (oder neuem Sub-Partial `layouts/_partials/_base/h-card.html`, eingebunden im Footer). Aktuell existiert dort eine "Article Time"-Logo/Follow-Icons-Spalte (Zeilen 24–34) — h-card-Block sitzt entweder als eigene Spalte daneben oder umschließt die existierende Identity-Sektion. Designer-Choice; keine Visual-Redesign-Pflicht.
- **Pre-Spec Note ergänzt:** "Codebase-Verifikation 2026-05-12: keine h-card im Repo. Story = Erstanlage, nicht Migration."
- **Pre-Spec Note ergänzt:** "Identity-Mismatch Phase 1: Site-Title ist `Article Time`, Footer-Sektion ebenfalls. h-card identifiziert dagegen die **Person** (Angel Crawford) — das ist IndieWeb-Konvention und unabhängig vom Site-Titel. Coexistenz von Site-Brand (`Article Time`) und Person-h-card (`Angel Crawford`) ist während Phase 1/2 zulässig."

**Vollständige finale AC-Liste (zur Bestätigung):**

```
1. h-card markup added to base layout (rendered on every page via footer-partial).
   Block contains: display name, profile photo, short bio, canonical self-link.
2. Display name marked with class="p-name".
3. Profile photo marked with class="u-photo" (existing site avatar; no new asset).
4. Short bio marked with class="p-note".
5. Canonical self-link rendered as <a class="u-url" rel="me" href="{{ .Site.BaseURL }}">.
   baseURL switch in Story 9.15 automatically re-points the href post-cutover.
6. Markup lives in layouts/_partials/_base/footer.html OR a new sub-partial
   layouts/_partials/_base/h-card.html included by the footer.
   Existing footer "Article Time" identity column (footer.html:24–34) is the
   coordination point — extend, wrap, or sit adjacent. No visual redesign required.
7. Content (name, bio text, photo path) sourced from config/_default/params.yaml
   (new params.identity block with keys: name, photo, note, url) —
   NOT hardcoded in the template.
8. indiewebify.me h-card test passes against any page URL during implementation
   (validates the markup, not the live domain).
9. No CSP changes required.
```

````markdown
## Story 9.14: Domain & Brand Migration Preparation

**As a** site owner
**I want** all preparatory work for the article-time.de → angel-crawford.de cutover AND the "Article Time" → "Angel Crawford" brand transition done BEFORE the cutover window
**So that** Story 9.15 (the actual cutover) is a deterministic execution of pre-staged changes, not live problem-solving under maintenance-mode pressure

**FR Coverage:** None — operational migration story per ADR.

**GitHub Issue:** TBD (fits under [#124 IndieWeb](https://github.com/AngelCrawford/blog/issues/124) umbrella).

**Source:** `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` § Phase 2 (Cutover Preparation) AND § Brand Identity Transition.

**Acceptance Criteria:**

**Domain track:**
1. **Maintenance-mode parking page prepared.** Static `index.html` written for post-cutover `article-time.de`. Plain HTML, no Hugo, no JS, no external deps. Copy per resolved ADR Open Question #3 (see AC8). File stored per AC7-decision location.
2. **Hardcoded-URL audit.** Sweep the repo for hardcoded `article-time.de` strings; commit inventory to `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` Implementation Tracking section. Per match: file path, line number(s), whether re-pointing is required at cutover or handled automatically by `baseURL`/`absURL`/`absLangURL`. Inventory must cover at least: `config/_default/config.yaml` (baseURL), `CNAME`, Hugo data files, templates with absolute URLs, content frontmatter with absolute `permalink`/`canonical`, `static/robots.txt`, webmention.io endpoint string in `layouts/_partials/_base/head.html`, and any `static/` HTML referencing `article-time.de`.
3. **Inventory cross-checked against epics-encoded AC URLs.** Stories 2.3 (AC1, AC2), 2.8 (AC1, AC3), 3.2 (AC1), 9.8 (AC7) reference `article-time.de` literally in `epics.md`. Inventory marks these as "epic-doc references — DO NOT edit retroactively; runtime re-pointing in Story 9.15." (See ADR § Consequences > Neutral.)
4. **GitHub Pages custom-domain change procedure documented.** Step-list (and/or screenshots) for `Settings → Pages → Custom domain` on both repos (blog repo AND `angel-crawford.de` profile-card repo) committed to the ADR.
5. **`CNAME` change procedure documented.** Current `CNAME` file location in blog repo recorded; target content (`angel-crawford.de`) noted.

**Brand track:**
6. **Final brand decisions recorded in ADR.** New ADR § Brand Identity Transition captures:
   - Final Site Title: exact string (e.g., `Angel Crawford`; optional tagline captured separately if desired).
   - Footer slogan replacement copy: replaces `footer.html:18–19` ("Du willst schreiben…Be a part of Article Time!"). Final wording approved.
   - Logo policy: clock-logo (`static/images/header/clock_small.webp`) replacement or removal decided; replacement asset path identified if applicable.
   - Personal avatar asset for `params.identity.photo` (Story 9.13 AC7): final Angel Crawford photo produced or sourced; path captured.
7. **Profile-card repo decision finalized.** ADR Open Question #1 resolved: archive read-only / delete entirely / repurpose as parking-page source. Decision + rationale recorded; determines parking-page hosting location (AC1).
8. **Parking-page copy approved.** Final wording recorded in ADR (Open Question #3 closed).

**Validation:**
9. **Pre-cutover validation in preview branch.** Build a preview/branch with: new Site Title, new logo (if any), new avatar, `params.identity` fully populated. Run `indiewebify.me` h-card test against the preview URL — confirms brand-transition assets do not break h-card markup from Story 9.13.

**Prerequisites:** Story 9.13 (Representative h-card in Base Layout) — DONE.

**Dependencies:** None.

**Effort:** 0.5–1 days (depending on whether brand-asset creation — new logo/avatar design — happens inside or outside this story; external design work, if needed, is outside-scope time).

**Out of Scope:**
- The cutover itself (Story 9.15).
- Retroactive edits to existing Story-2.x or Story-3.2 ACs in epics.md (Story 9.15 re-points them at the code level only).
- DNS work (both domains already resolve via GitHub Pages — per ADR).
- 301 redirect setup (no inbound URLs to preserve — per ADR).
````

## Edit-Proposal 3 — Story 9.15 (NEU)

**Insertion-Punkt:** Direkt nach Story 9.14.

````markdown
## Story 9.15: Domain & Brand Migration Cutover

**As a** site owner
**I want** the blog deployment switched from article-time.de to angel-crawford.de AND the site brand switched from "Article Time" to "Angel Crawford" in one deterministic execution window
**So that** the IndieWeb identity becomes coherent (one domain = one brand = one person) and the old profile-card site is retired in the same act

**FR Coverage:** None — operational migration story per ADR.

**GitHub Issue:** TBD (fits under [#124 IndieWeb](https://github.com/AngelCrawford/blog/issues/124) umbrella).

**Source:** `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` § Phase 3 AND § Brand Identity Transition.

**Acceptance Criteria:** *(execution order matters — follow numbered sequence)*

**Pre-flight:**
1. **Activate maintenance-mode on blog.** `echo "" > .maintenance && git tag → push` (per README → Maintenance Mode). Verify maintenance page renders on `article-time.de` before proceeding.

**Domain config (single commit):**
2. **Update `baseURL`.** `config/_default/config.yaml` → `baseURL: "https://angel-crawford.de"`.
3. **Update `CNAME` file.** Blog repo top-level `CNAME` → `angel-crawford.de`.

**Brand config (same commit as 2 + 3 is acceptable):**
4. **Update Site Title.** `config/_default/config.yaml` → `title: "Angel Crawford"` (or final string per Story 9.14 AC6).
5. **Replace footer slogan.** `layouts/_partials/_base/footer.html` lines 18–19 ("Du willst schreiben…Be a part of Article Time!") → final copy per 9.14 AC6.
6. **Swap logo asset (if decided in 9.14 AC6).** Replace/remove `static/images/header/clock_small.webp` and any references; ensure new logo path is wired into templates (footer.html:26 and any other usage).
7. **Verify `params.identity` populated.** Story 9.13's `params.identity.photo` must point at final Angel Crawford avatar from 9.14 AC6 (not placeholder).

**Re-point hardcoded `article-time.de` in active code (inventory from 9.14 AC2):**
8. **Apply re-point inventory.** Walk the inventory committed in Story 9.14 AC2 and edit each flagged code location. Specifically (non-exhaustive — full list lives in ADR Implementation Tracking):
   - `layouts/_partials/_base/head.html` — webmention.io endpoint URL (the code instance of Story 2.3 AC1).
   - `scripts/fetch-webmentions.js` (or equivalent) — `domain=article-time.de` curl param (code instance of Story 3.2 AC1, when that story is implemented).
   - Any other code locations flagged by the inventory.
9. **Epic-doc references intentionally untouched.** Story 2.3/2.8/3.2/9.8 ACs in `epics.md` still reference `article-time.de` literally. Per ADR Consequences > Neutral, this is intentional — `epics.md` is a planning snapshot, not a runtime artifact. No retroactive edit.

**Deployment switch:**
10. **GitHub Pages custom-domain change (blog repo).** `Settings → Pages → Custom domain` → `angel-crawford.de`. Wait for green "DNS check successful".
11. **Retire old profile-card repo.** Execute the route chosen in Story 9.14 AC7 (archive / delete / repurpose-as-parking-page-source).
12. **Re-register webmention.io endpoint.** Log into webmention.io, register `angel-crawford.de` (new endpoint: `https://webmention.io/angel-crawford.de/webmention`). Update endpoint URL anywhere it's hardcoded post-AC8. Per ADR: dropping `article-time.de` webmention.io history is acceptable (no live mentions to preserve).
13. **Update Bridgy.fed domain verification.** Re-verify Bridgy account against `angel-crawford.de`. Document the new verification artifact (rel="me" or webfinger path) in the runbook.
14. **Deploy parking page to `article-time.de`.** Whichever GitHub Pages repo serves `article-time.de` now → confirm parking-page `index.html` from Story 9.14 AC1 is live. `article-time.de` shows "moved" copy.

**Post-flight:**
15. **Deactivate maintenance-mode on blog.** Remove `.maintenance` toggle, tag/push per README.
16. **Validation pass:**
    - Mastodon `rel="me"` verification still green on `norden.social/@Angel_Crawford_ftw` (target domain unchanged from Mastodon's side — should be transparent).
    - `indiewebify.me` h-card AND h-entry tests both pass on `https://angel-crawford.de/`.
    - Blog loads at `https://angel-crawford.de/`, returns 200, content renders, h-card self-link href is `https://angel-crawford.de/` (auto-resolved from new `baseURL` per Story 9.13 AC5).
    - `https://article-time.de/` returns the parking page.
    - Smoke test: send a test webmention from webmention.rocks targeting an article on `angel-crawford.de` → arrives in webmention.io dashboard within minutes.
17. **Update ADR status.** `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` → Status: `Accepted` → `Implemented`, with implementation date.

**Prerequisites:**
- Story 9.13 (Representative h-card in Base Layout) — DONE.
- Story 9.14 (Domain & Brand Migration Preparation) — DONE; all preparatory artifacts in place (parking page, inventory, brand assets, decisions).

**Dependencies:** None — but coordinate with Epic 7 (POSSE) if it's in flight: any Mastodon-API integration must target `angel-crawford.de` post-cutover.

**Effort:** 0.5–1 days (execution window; preparatory work all in 9.14).

**Implementation Note:** This is a coordinated execution story, not a code-design story. "Coding" is small (text edits per AC2–8); weight is in the deterministic execution sequence + validation pass at AC16. Recommend low-traffic window (weekend morning German time).

**Out of Scope:**
- 301 redirects from `article-time.de/*` to `angel-crawford.de/*` — per ADR, no inbound links to preserve. If this changes between now and cutover (some external link points at an `article-time.de` URL), the parking page can be enhanced post-cutover with `<meta refresh>` or per-URL redirects.
- `article-time.de` domain registration changes — domain stays registered per ADR § Consequences > Negative.
````

### Diff 2 — Neuer Abschnitt `Brand Identity Transition`

**Insertion-Punkt:** Vor § Sequencing (vor Zeile 96 `## Sequencing (Risk-Ordered)`).

````markdown
## Brand Identity Transition

The domain migration is the **outer** half of the identity-coherence
work. The **inner** half is the brand identity itself: the site
currently presents as "Article Time" (Site Title, hero brand text,
footer slogan, clock-logo), which is the magazine-project name, not
the personal-identity name. Domain coherence without brand coherence
would leave a residual mismatch — `angel-crawford.de` serving a site
titled "Article Time".

Brand transition is therefore scoped into the same migration, not
deferred. The transition is executed in the same Phase 2 / Phase 3
sequencing as the domain move, so cutover delivers a single coherent
identity in one window.

### What changes

- **Site Title:** `Article Time` → `Angel Crawford` (exact string
  decided in Story 9.14 AC6; tagline optional).
- **Footer slogan** (`layouts/_partials/_base/footer.html` lines
  18–19, "Du willst schreiben — ohne einen eignen Blog zu
  veröffentlichen? Be a part of Article Time!") → final copy decided
  in Story 9.14 AC6.
- **Logo asset:** `static/images/header/clock_small.webp` is the
  Article-Time clock-logo (brand artwork for the magazine concept).
  Replacement or removal decided in Story 9.14 AC6.
- **Personal avatar:** does not currently exist as a site asset. Must
  be produced/sourced and registered at `params.identity.photo` (per
  Story 9.13 AC7). Decided in Story 9.14 AC6.

### What stays

- **`params.author.name`** in the footer copyright block already
  resolves to "Angel Crawford" via the author param fallback chain
  (`footer.html:102`). No change.
- **Mastodon handle** `norden.social/@Angel_Crawford_ftw` and all
  silo `rel="me"` links remain unchanged — they already target the
  person, not the brand.
- **Content** (articles, logs, taxonomies) — no rename pass over
  existing content; the brand transition is structural, not content.

### Tolerated interim mismatch

During Phase 1 (build) and most of Phase 2 (prep), the site continues
to present as "Article Time" while Story 9.13's h-card already
identifies "Angel Crawford" as the person behind the site. This is a
**tolerated mismatch** — IndieWeb-conformant (the h-card describes the
person, not the brand), and intentional: brand-asset work is staged
inside Story 9.14 (Prep), and the brand switch happens in lockstep
with the domain switch in Story 9.15 (Cutover) so there is no period
where the domain is `angel-crawford.de` while the brand says "Article
Time".
````

```
## Story 9.13: Representative h-card in Base Layout

**As a** site owner
**I want** a full h-card microformat in the base layout (rendered site-wide via the footer partial)
**So that** IndieWeb tooling (webmention.io, Bridgy.fed, Mastodon rel="me" verification, indiewebify.me) treats the personal-identity domain as a coherent IndieWeb identity surface

**FR Coverage:** None directly — IndieWeb identity infrastructure that the engagement/POSSE epics depend on. Closest match: FR-011, FR-012 (webmentions assume identity coherence).

**GitHub Issue:** TBD (fits under [#124 IndieWeb](https://github.com/AngelCrawford/blog/issues/124) umbrella).

**Source:** Gap identified in `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` § Follow-up task. ADR concluded that no existing story (9.2 Schema.org JSON-LD, 9.10 Author-Box on article pages, 9.12 Social-Follow icon row) covers full h-card microformat markup site-wide. Codebase verification on 2026-05-12 confirmed: no h-card markup exists anywhere in the repo. This story is first-time creation, not migration. The blog-side h-card is the artefact that replaces the old profile card on `angel-crawford.de` during the Phase 3 cutover (Story 9.15). Without it, Phase 3 ships an h-card-less site under the personal-identity domain.

**Acceptance Criteria:**
1. h-card markup added to base layout (rendered on every page via footer-partial). Block contains: display name, profile photo, short bio, canonical self-link.
2. Display name marked with `class="p-name"`.
3. Profile photo marked with `class="u-photo"`. NEW ASSET: site currently has no personal avatar — the Article Time clock-logo (`static/images/header/clock_small.webp`) is brand artwork, not a personal photo. A new Angel Crawford avatar must be added (path stored in `params.identity.photo` per AC7). Acceptable interim: a placeholder avatar can be used during implementation if a final photo isn't ready, with a TODO in completion notes to swap to final asset before Story 9.15 cutover.
4. Short bio marked with `class="p-note"`.
5. Canonical self-link rendered as `<a class="u-url" rel="me" href="{{ .Site.BaseURL }}">…</a>`. Phase 1: Hugo resolves to `article-time.de`. Phase 3: after `baseURL` update in Story 9.15, automatically resolves to `angel-crawford.de`. No code-touch in 9.15 required for this link.
6. Markup lives in `layouts/_partials/_base/footer.html` OR a new sub-partial `layouts/_partials/_base/h-card.html` included by the footer. Existing footer "Article Time" identity column (`footer.html` lines 24–34) is the coordination point — extend, wrap, or sit adjacent. Implementer-choice; no visual redesign required.
7. Content (name, bio text, photo path) sourced from `config/_default/params.yaml` (new `params.identity` block with keys: `name`, `photo`, `note`, `url`) — NOT hardcoded in the template.
8. `indiewebify.me` h-card test passes against any page URL during implementation (validates the markup, not the live domain).
9. No CSP changes required (no new external dependencies).

**Prerequisites:** None.

**Dependencies:**
- Soft coordination with Story 9.12 (Social-Follow Icon Row). Once 9.12 lands, the `params.social`-driven `rel="me"` silo links should sit INSIDE the h-card wrapper to count as h-card-relative `rel="me"` links. If 9.13 lands before 9.12: implement only the canonical self-link (AC5); the silo `rel="me"` links are added by 9.12 inside the same wrapper.
- Soft coordination with Story 9.10 (Author-Box on article pages). 9.10 renders an author block at article footer (`<aside>`), not site-wide — no direct conflict. If 9.10 ALSO adopts h-card classes for the per-article author, that is valid microformats2 (multiple h-cards per site are allowed).

**Effort:** 0.5 days

**Pre-Spec Notes:**
- **Codebase verification 2026-05-12.** Grep for `h-card|p-name|u-photo|p-note|u-url|h-entry|p-author|microformat` returned no real matches (only Bulma `button.scss` class names and unrelated BMAD tooling). Story is first-time h-card creation, not refactor.
- **Single-author identity surface.** The site-wide h-card identifies the ONE site owner. This story is unaffected by the separate Multi-Author → Co-Author decision (deferred to its own ADR) — per-article co-author markup is a property of the h-entry on article pages (Story 9.10 / 9.2 territory), not of this site-wide h-card.
- **Self-link absolute via `baseURL`, not relative.** AC5 must use `{{ .Site.BaseURL }}`, not `/`. Microformats2 parsers and IndieWeb tooling do resolve relative URLs in most cases, but `rel="me"` round-trip validators have known issues with relative `href` — absolute keeps the verification path robust AND lets Story 9.15's `baseURL` switch carry the domain change automatically.
- **Brand vs. person separation.** The h-card identifies the person (Angel Crawford), not the site brand (currently "Article Time", transitioning to "Angel Crawford" per ADR § Brand Identity Transition). h-card content is sourced from `params.identity` (the person), not from `Site.Title` (the brand). This makes the h-card stable across the brand transition — no AC change required in 9.15. During Phase 1/2 the site brand says "Article Time" while the h-card identifies "Angel Crawford"; this is a tolerated interim mismatch per ADR.

## Story 9.14: Domain & Brand Migration Preparation

**As a** site owner
**I want** all preparatory work for the article-time.de → angel-crawford.de cutover AND the "Article Time" → "Angel Crawford" brand transition done BEFORE the cutover window
**So that** Story 9.15 (the actual cutover) is a deterministic execution of pre-staged changes, not live problem-solving under maintenance-mode pressure

**FR Coverage:** None — operational migration story per ADR.

**GitHub Issue:** TBD (fits under [#124 IndieWeb](https://github.com/AngelCrawford/blog/issues/124) umbrella).

**Source:** `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` § Phase 2 (Cutover Preparation) AND § Brand Identity Transition.

**Acceptance Criteria:**

**Domain track:**
1. **Maintenance-mode parking page prepared.** Static `index.html` written for post-cutover `article-time.de`. Plain HTML, no Hugo, no JS, no external deps. Copy per resolved ADR Open Question #3 (see AC8). File stored per AC7-decision location.
2. **Hardcoded-URL audit.** Sweep the repo for hardcoded `article-time.de` strings; commit inventory to `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` Implementation Tracking section. Per match: file path, line number(s), whether re-pointing is required at cutover or handled automatically by `baseURL`/`absURL`/`absLangURL`. Inventory must cover at least: `config/_default/config.yaml` (baseURL), `CNAME`, Hugo data files, templates with absolute URLs, content frontmatter with absolute `permalink`/`canonical`, `static/robots.txt`, webmention.io endpoint string in `layouts/_partials/_base/head.html`, and any `static/` HTML referencing `article-time.de`.
3. **Inventory cross-checked against epics-encoded AC URLs.** Stories 2.3 (AC1, AC2), 2.8 (AC1, AC3), 3.2 (AC1), 9.8 (AC7) reference `article-time.de` literally in `epics.md`. Inventory marks these as "epic-doc references — DO NOT edit retroactively; runtime re-pointing in Story 9.15." (See ADR § Consequences > Neutral.)
4. **GitHub Pages custom-domain change procedure documented.** Step-list (and/or screenshots) for `Settings → Pages → Custom domain` on both repos (blog repo AND `angel-crawford.de` profile-card repo) committed to the ADR.
5. **`CNAME` change procedure documented.** Current `CNAME` file location in blog repo recorded; target content (`angel-crawford.de`) noted.

**Brand track:**
6. **Final brand decisions recorded in ADR.** ADR § Brand Identity Transition captures:
   - Final Site Title: exact string (e.g., `Angel Crawford`; optional tagline captured separately if desired).
   - Footer slogan replacement copy: replaces `footer.html` lines 18–19 ("Du willst schreiben…Be a part of Article Time!"). Final wording approved.
   - Logo policy: clock-logo (`static/images/header/clock_small.webp`) replacement or removal decided; replacement asset path identified if applicable.
   - Personal avatar asset for `params.identity.photo` (Story 9.13 AC7): final Angel Crawford photo produced or sourced; path captured.
7. **Profile-card repo decision finalized.** ADR Open Question #1 resolved: archive read-only / delete entirely / repurpose as parking-page source. Decision + rationale recorded; determines parking-page hosting location (AC1).
8. **Parking-page copy approved.** Final wording recorded in ADR (Open Question #3 closed).

**Validation:**
9. **Pre-cutover validation in preview branch.** Build a preview/branch with: new Site Title, new logo (if any), new avatar, `params.identity` fully populated. Run `indiewebify.me` h-card test against the preview URL — confirms brand-transition assets do not break h-card markup from Story 9.13.

**Prerequisites:** Story 9.13 (Representative h-card in Base Layout) — DONE.

**Dependencies:** None.

**Effort:** 0.5–1 days (depending on whether brand-asset creation — new logo/avatar design — happens inside or outside this story; external design work, if needed, is outside-scope time).

**Out of Scope:**
- The cutover itself (Story 9.15).
- Retroactive edits to existing Story-2.x or Story-3.2 ACs in `epics.md` (Story 9.15 re-points them at the code level only).
- DNS work (both domains already resolve via GitHub Pages — per ADR).
- 301 redirect setup (no inbound URLs to preserve — per ADR).

## Story 9.15: Domain & Brand Migration Cutover

**As a** site owner
**I want** the blog deployment switched from article-time.de to angel-crawford.de AND the site brand switched from "Article Time" to "Angel Crawford" in one deterministic execution window
**So that** the IndieWeb identity becomes coherent (one domain = one brand = one person) and the old profile-card site is retired in the same act

**FR Coverage:** None — operational migration story per ADR.

**GitHub Issue:** TBD (fits under [#124 IndieWeb](https://github.com/AngelCrawford/blog/issues/124) umbrella).

**Source:** `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` § Phase 3 AND § Brand Identity Transition.

**Acceptance Criteria:** *(execution order matters — follow numbered sequence)*

**Pre-flight:**
1. **Activate maintenance-mode on blog.** `echo "" > .maintenance && git tag → push` (per README → Maintenance Mode). Verify maintenance page renders on `article-time.de` before proceeding.

**Domain config (single commit):**
2. **Update `baseURL`.** `config/_default/config.yaml` → `baseURL: "https://angel-crawford.de"`.
3. **Update `CNAME` file.** Blog repo top-level `CNAME` → `angel-crawford.de`.

**Brand config (same commit as 2 + 3 is acceptable):**
4. **Update Site Title.** `config/_default/config.yaml` → `title: "Angel Crawford"` (or final string per Story 9.14 AC6).
5. **Replace footer slogan.** `layouts/_partials/_base/footer.html` lines 18–19 ("Du willst schreiben…Be a part of Article Time!") → final copy per 9.14 AC6.
6. **Swap logo asset (if decided in 9.14 AC6).** Replace/remove `static/images/header/clock_small.webp` and any references; ensure new logo path is wired into templates (`footer.html` line 26 and any other usage).
7. **Verify `params.identity` populated.** Story 9.13's `params.identity.photo` must point at final Angel Crawford avatar from 9.14 AC6 (not placeholder).

**Re-point hardcoded `article-time.de` in active code (inventory from 9.14 AC2):**
8. **Apply re-point inventory.** Walk the inventory committed in Story 9.14 AC2 and edit each flagged code location. Specifically (non-exhaustive — full list lives in ADR Implementation Tracking):
   - `layouts/_partials/_base/head.html` — webmention.io endpoint URL (the code instance of Story 2.3 AC1).
   - `scripts/fetch-webmentions.js` (or equivalent) — `domain=article-time.de` curl param (code instance of Story 3.2 AC1, when that story is implemented).
   - Any other code locations flagged by the inventory.
9. **Epic-doc references intentionally untouched.** Story 2.3/2.8/3.2/9.8 ACs in `epics.md` still reference `article-time.de` literally. Per ADR § Consequences > Neutral, this is intentional — `epics.md` is a planning snapshot, not a runtime artifact. Git history is the audit trail; no retroactive edit.

**Deployment switch:**
10. **GitHub Pages custom-domain change (blog repo).** `Settings → Pages → Custom domain` → `angel-crawford.de`. Wait for green "DNS check successful".
11. **Retire old profile-card repo.** Execute the route chosen in Story 9.14 AC7 (archive / delete / repurpose-as-parking-page-source).
12. **Re-register webmention.io endpoint.** Log into webmention.io, register `angel-crawford.de` (new endpoint: `https://webmention.io/angel-crawford.de/webmention`). Update endpoint URL anywhere it's hardcoded post-AC8. Per ADR: dropping `article-time.de` webmention.io history is acceptable (no live mentions to preserve).
13. **Update Bridgy.fed domain verification.** Re-verify Bridgy account against `angel-crawford.de`. Document the new verification artifact (`rel="me"` or webfinger path) in the runbook.
14. **Deploy parking page to `article-time.de`.** Whichever GitHub Pages repo serves `article-time.de` now → confirm parking-page `index.html` from Story 9.14 AC1 is live. `article-time.de` shows "moved" copy.

**Post-flight:**
15. **Deactivate maintenance-mode on blog.** Remove `.maintenance` toggle, tag/push per README.
16. **Validation pass:**
    - Mastodon `rel="me"` verification still green on `norden.social/@Angel_Crawford_ftw` (target domain unchanged from Mastodon's side — should be transparent).
    - `indiewebify.me` h-card AND h-entry tests both pass on `https://angel-crawford.de/`.
    - Blog loads at `https://angel-crawford.de/`, returns 200, content renders, h-card self-link `href` is `https://angel-crawford.de/` (auto-resolved from new `baseURL` per Story 9.13 AC5).
    - `https://article-time.de/` returns the parking page.
    - Smoke test: send a test webmention from webmention.rocks targeting an article on `angel-crawford.de` → arrives in webmention.io dashboard within minutes.
17. **Update ADR status.** `docs/2-solutioning/adr-domain-migration.md` → Status: `Accepted` → `Implemented`, with implementation date.

**Prerequisites:**
- Story 9.13 (Representative h-card in Base Layout) — DONE.
- Story 9.14 (Domain & Brand Migration Preparation) — DONE; all preparatory artifacts in place (parking page, inventory, brand assets, decisions).

**Dependencies:** None — but coordinate with Epic 7 (POSSE) if it's in flight: any Mastodon-API integration must target `angel-crawford.de` post-cutover.

**Effort:** 0.5–1 days (execution window; preparatory work all in Story 9.14).

**Implementation Note:** This is a coordinated execution story, not a code-design story. "Coding" is small (text edits per AC2–8); weight is in the deterministic execution sequence + validation pass at AC16. Recommend low-traffic window (weekend morning German time).

**Out of Scope:**
- 301 redirects from `article-time.de/*` to `angel-crawford.de/*` — per ADR, no inbound links to preserve. If this changes between now and cutover (some external link points at an `article-time.de` URL), the parking page can be enhanced post-cutover with `<meta refresh>` or per-URL redirects.
- `article-time.de` domain registration changes — domain stays registered per ADR § Consequences > Negative.
```

```
# ADR: Domain Migration article-time.de → angel-crawford.de

**Status:** Accepted
**Date:** 2026-05-12
**Decided:** 2026-05-12
**Owner:** Angel Crawford
**Related:** [`docs/1-planning/epics.md`](../1-planning/epics.md), Epic 2 (IndieWeb infrastructure)

```

Status: Completed

Diff: D:\Projects\Hugo\Sites\blog\docs\2-solutioning\adr-domain-migration.md

```
---

## Brand Identity Transition

The domain migration is the **outer** half of the identity-coherence
work. The **inner** half is the brand identity itself: the site
currently presents as "Article Time" (Site Title, hero brand text,
footer slogan, clock-logo), which is the magazine-project name, not
the personal-identity name. Domain coherence without brand coherence
would leave a residual mismatch — `angel-crawford.de` serving a site
titled "Article Time".

Brand transition is therefore scoped into the same migration, not
deferred. The transition is executed in the same Phase 2 / Phase 3
sequencing as the domain move, so cutover delivers a single coherent
identity in one window.

### What changes

- **Site Title:** `Article Time` → `Angel Crawford` (exact string
  decided in Story 9.14 AC6; tagline optional).
- **Footer slogan** (`layouts/_partials/_base/footer.html` lines
  18–19, "Du willst schreiben — ohne einen eignen Blog zu
  veröffentlichen? Be a part of Article Time!") → final copy decided
  in Story 9.14 AC6.
- **Logo asset:** `static/images/header/clock_small.webp` is the
  Article-Time clock-logo (brand artwork for the magazine concept).
  Replacement or removal decided in Story 9.14 AC6.
- **Personal avatar:** does not currently exist as a site asset. Must
  be produced/sourced and registered at `params.identity.photo` (per
  Story 9.13 AC7). Decided in Story 9.14 AC6.

### What stays

- **`params.author.name`** in the footer copyright block already
  resolves to "Angel Crawford" via the author param fallback chain
  (`footer.html` line 102). No change.
- **Mastodon handle** `norden.social/@Angel_Crawford_ftw` and all
  silo `rel="me"` links remain unchanged — they already target the
  person, not the brand.
- **Content** (articles, logs, taxonomies) — no rename pass over
  existing content; the brand transition is structural, not content.

### Tolerated interim mismatch

During Phase 1 (build) and most of Phase 2 (prep), the site continues
to present as "Article Time" while Story 9.13's h-card already
identifies "Angel Crawford" as the person behind the site. This is a
**tolerated mismatch** — IndieWeb-conformant (the h-card describes the
person, not the brand), and intentional: brand-asset work is staged
inside Story 9.14 (Prep), and the brand switch happens in lockstep
with the domain switch in Story 9.15 (Cutover) so there is no period
where the domain is `angel-crawford.de` while the brand says "Article
Time".
```

```
## Implementation Tracking

This ADR adds three new Stories to Epic 9:

- **Story 9.13: Representative h-card in Base Layout** — foundation
  artifact (site-wide h-card identifying Angel Crawford as the person
  behind the site). Prereq for the cutover; built ahead of time so
  cutover does not have to ship h-card markup under maintenance-mode
  pressure. Verified at draft time: no h-card markup exists anywhere
  in the codebase, so this is first-time creation, not migration.
- **Story 9.14: Domain & Brand Migration Preparation** — Phase 2
  preparatory work plus brand-asset preparation (final Site Title,
  footer slogan, logo policy, personal avatar). Resolves Open
  Questions #1 and #3 below.
- **Story 9.15: Domain & Brand Migration Cutover** — Phase 3 cutover
  execution PLUS brand switch in the same commit. Final ship-blocker
  before declaring 1.0. On completion, this ADR's status flips to
  `Implemented`.

Story 9.13 is a Foundation Story (no prerequisites). Story 9.14
requires 9.13. Story 9.15 requires both 9.13 and 9.14.

Inventory of hardcoded `article-time.de` strings in the codebase
(produced by Story 9.14 AC2) will be appended to this section once
that story runs. The inventory is the work list for Story 9.15 AC8.
```

```
1	# Story 2.8: Bridgy.fed Setup & Verification

5	## Story
6	
7	As a content creator,
8	I want Mastodon (and Fediverse) replies, boosts, and likes to flow into my site as webmentions,
9	so that federated engagement actually shows up in the webmention display, not just the small IndieWeb-blog subset.
10	
11	## Acceptance Criteria
12	
13	1. **Bridgy Fed account connected to a chosen Mastodon handle representing article-time.de.** The chosen Fediverse handle (e.g. `@article-time@mastodon.social`) and home instance are documented in `docs/technical/runbook.md` under a new `## Bridgy.fed (Fediverse Bridge)` section so a future operator (or future-Angel) knows where the bridge lives. **Mastodon-only for this story** (Bluesky out of scope per Out-of-Scope below). The story does **not** prescribe the instance — Angel picks at execution time and the chosen value is captured in the runbook entry + this story's Completion Notes. If a per-domain handle is preferred (`@me@article-time.de` via custom domain federation), document that path instead, but the simpler `@<handle>@<existing-instance>` route is the default.

47	
48	**Convention reconciliation (AC #2 — verification mechanism):** The epics AC says "rel='me' link **or** `.well-known/webfinger` — choose whichever Bridgy currently requires; document the chosen path." Decision: **rel="me"**. Reasons: (a) Bridgy Fed's current docs accept it; (b) GitHub Pages serves static files but `.well-known/` doesn't have any project precedent (would require a `static/.well-known/webfinger` file + JSON content-type negotiation that Hugo's static-file pipeline does not auto-set, requiring a custom `_headers` or wrapping route); (c) rel="me" matches the pattern Story 2.3 already established for webmention.io's IndieAuth verification; (d) Story 9.12's planned `params.social`-driven `rel="me"` set absorbs the Mastodon line cleanly, so the shim has a documented retirement path. Document the choice in Completion Notes.
49	
50	**Convention reconciliation (AC #4 — Privacy policy section structure):** The existing `## Webmentions` section uses **bold-label paragraphs** (e.g. `**Verarbeitung durch webmention.io:**`) for sub-sections, NOT `###` headings. This is different from Story 2.3's draft (which used `### Verarbeitung durch webmention.io`) but matches the **as-shipped** state after Story 2.5's privacy-policy refresh consolidated the structure. The Bridgy sub-section added by this story follows the **as-shipped bold-label** convention, not the Story-2.3-draft `###` convention. Verify by re-reading `content/pages/datenschutz.md` at implementation time.
51	
52	**Convention reconciliation (AC #5 — runbook location):** The epics AC names `docs/technical/runbook.md` as the documentation target. This file exists (132 lines, established by prior epics) and is the canonical operator reference per README line 10 ("Operations runbook"). Insert new section between `## Deploy` and EOF — same end-of-file pattern existing sections use. Do NOT create a separate `docs/technical/bridgy.md` or `docs/technical/operations.md` — runbook is the single-source-of-truth file per project convention.
53	
54	**Convention reconciliation (no `params.yaml` block for Bridgy):** Bridgy's setup is **entirely external** — there is no API key, no domain config, no script URL the site renders. Unlike Umami (which has `params.umami.{website_id, script_url}` blocks because the site emits Umami's script tag) or webmention.io (which has its endpoint URL hardcoded in head.html), Bridgy is a server-to-server service that posts mentions to webmention.io without the site ever talking to it. **No `params.yaml` entry is needed or created.** The Mastodon handle URL is hardcoded into the `rel="me"` shim — same single-tenant pattern Story 2.3 used for the webmention endpoint URL.
```
